        true, true, true, true, true, true, true, true,
        true, true, true, true, true, true, true, true,
        true, true, true, true, true, true, true, true,
    ];

    public static int Run(int input)
    {
        var digits = Digits[input];
        var mode = Modes[input];
        if (Widths[input] == 32)
        {
            var value = BitConverter.UInt32BitsToSingle((uint)Sources[input]);
            var result = mode == 5 ? MathF.Round(value, digits) : MathF.Round(value, digits, (MidpointRounding)mode);
            if (ExpectNaN[input])
                return float.IsNaN(result) ? 42 : -1;
            return BitConverter.SingleToUInt32Bits(result) == Expected[input] ? 42 : -2;
        }
        else
        {
            var value = BitConverter.UInt64BitsToDouble(Sources[input]);
            var result = mode == 5 ? Math.Round(value, digits) : Math.Round(value, digits, (MidpointRounding)mode);
            if (ExpectNaN[input])
                return double.IsNaN(result) ? 42 : -1;
            return BitConverter.DoubleToUInt64Bits(result) == Expected[input] ? 42 : -2;
        }
    }
}
